    <script id="smmprime-ios-dock-taps">
      (function () {
        'use strict';
        var ios = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) ||
          (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);
        var standalone = navigator.standalone === true ||
          window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches;
        if (!ios || !standalone) return;
        var dock = document.querySelector('.reg-menu__short.sp-dock');
        if (!dock) return;
        var tap = null;
        var lastItem = null;
        var ignoreClickUntil = 0;
        function itemAt(target) {
          var item = target && target.closest && target.closest('.sp-dock__item');
          return item && dock.contains(item) ? item : null;
        }
        function reset() { tap = null; }
        dock.addEventListener('touchstart', function (event) {
          reset();
          if (event.touches.length !== 1) return;
          var item = itemAt(event.target);
          if (!item) return;
          var touch = event.touches[0];
          tap = { item: item, id: touch.identifier, x: touch.clientX,
            y: touch.clientY, time: Date.now() };
        }, { passive: true });
        dock.addEventListener('touchmove', function (event) {
          if (!tap) return;
          var touch = event.touches[0];
          if (event.touches.length !== 1 || touch.identifier !== tap.id ||
              Math.abs(touch.clientX - tap.x) > 10 ||
              Math.abs(touch.clientY - tap.y) > 10) reset();
        }, { passive: true });
        dock.addEventListener('touchcancel', reset, { passive: true });
        dock.addEventListener('touchend', function (event) {
          var current = tap;
          reset();
          if (!current || event.touches.length || !event.cancelable ||
              event.defaultPrevented || Date.now() - current.time > 600) return;
          var touch = event.changedTouches[0];
          if (!touch || touch.identifier !== current.id ||
              Math.abs(touch.clientX - current.x) > 10 ||
              Math.abs(touch.clientY - current.y) > 10 ||
              itemAt(document.elementFromPoint(touch.clientX, touch.clientY)) !== current.item) return;
          var item = current.item;
          if (item.tagName === 'A') {
            var url = new URL(item.href, window.location.href);
            if (url.origin !== window.location.origin || item.hasAttribute('download') ||
                (item.target && item.target !== '_self')) return;
          }
          // Cancel the delayed compatibility click; activate exactly once.
          event.preventDefault();
          lastItem = item;
          ignoreClickUntil = Date.now() + 800;
          if (item.tagName === 'A') window.location.assign(item.href);
          else item.click(); // Reuse the existing Menu toggle, without a second handler.
        }, { passive: false });
        dock.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
          // Preserve keyboard/VoiceOver/programmatic activation (detail === 0).
          if (event.detail !== 0 && Date.now() < ignoreClickUntil &&
              itemAt(event.target) === lastItem) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopImmediatePropagation();
          }
        }, true);
        window.addEventListener('pageshow', function () {
          reset();
          lastItem = null;
          ignoreClickUntil = 0;
        });
      }());
    </script>

      <script id="smmprime-guest-social-dropdown-control">
      (function () {
        var strip = document.querySelector('.sp-guest-shell .sp-social-strip');
        if (!strip) return;
        var desktop = window.matchMedia('(min-width:1101px)');
        var triggers = Array.prototype.slice.call(strip.querySelectorAll('.sp-social-strip__link:not(.sp-social-strip__free)'));
        var panels = strip.querySelectorAll('.sp-social-dropdown');
        var active = -1, timer;
        function close() {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          panels.forEach(function (panel) { panel.hidden = true; });
          triggers.forEach(function (link) { if (desktop.matches) link.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); });
          active = -1;
        }
        function open(index) {
          if (!desktop.matches) return;
          close(); active = index;
          panels[index].hidden = false;
          var rect = strip.getBoundingClientRect();
          var triggerRect = triggers[index].getBoundingClientRect();
          var width = panels[index].getBoundingClientRect().width;
          var left = Math.max(24, Math.min(triggerRect.left - rect.left + triggerRect.width / 2 - width / 2, rect.width - width - 24));
          panels[index].style.left = left + 'px';
          panels[index].style.transform = 'none';
          triggers[index].setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        }
        function sync() {
          close();
          triggers.forEach(function (link, index) {
            if (desktop.matches) {
              link.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
              link.setAttribute('aria-controls', panels[index].id);
            } else {
              link.removeAttribute('aria-expanded'); link.removeAttribute('aria-controls');
            }
          });
        }
        triggers.forEach(function (link, index) {
          link.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { open(index); });
          link.addEventListener('focus', function () { open(index); });
          link.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
            if (!desktop.matches || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey || event.altKey) return;
            event.preventDefault(); open(index);
          });
          link.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
            if (!desktop.matches || event.key !== 'ArrowDown') return;
            event.preventDefault(); open(index); panels[index].querySelector('a').focus();
          });
        });
        strip.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { clearTimeout(timer); });
        strip.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () { timer = setTimeout(close, 180); });
        strip.addEventListener('focusout', function (event) { if (!strip.contains(event.relatedTarget)) close(); });
        document.addEventListener('click', function (event) { if (!strip.contains(event.target)) close(); });
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
          if (event.key === 'Escape' && active >= 0) { var link = triggers[active]; link.focus(); close(); }
        });
        if (desktop.addEventListener) desktop.addEventListener('change', sync);
        else desktop.addListener(sync);
        window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (active >= 0) open(active); });
        sync();
      }());
      </script>

      </div>
      <script id="smmprime-guest-shell-size">
        (function () {
          var shell = document.querySelector('.sp-guest-shell');
          var space = document.querySelector('.sp-guest-space');
          var strip = shell.querySelector('.sp-social-strip');
          var desktop = window.matchMedia('(min-width:1101px)');
          function update() {
            // Mobile pages already reserve the original header space.
            // Add only the new strip; desktop reserves both complete rows.
            var shellHeight = Math.ceil(shell.getBoundingClientRect().height);
            var stripHeight = desktop.matches ? shellHeight : Math.ceil(strip.getBoundingClientRect().height);
            var spaceHeight = stripHeight + 'px';
            if (space.style.height !== spaceHeight) space.style.height = spaceHeight;
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sp-guest-anchor-offset', shellHeight + 12 + 'px');
            document.documentElement.classList.add('sp-guest-anchor-offset');
          }
          update();
          if ('ResizeObserver' in window) {
            var observer = new ResizeObserver(update);
            observer.observe(shell); observer.observe(strip);
          }
          window.addEventListener('resize', update);
          window.addEventListener('load', update);
          window.addEventListener('pageshow', update);
          if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(update);
          var path = window.location.pathname.replace(/\/$/, '') || '/';
          strip.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (link) {
            if (link.getAttribute('href') !== '/services' && link.getAttribute('href') === path) link.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
          });
        }());
      </script>
      <style id="smmprime-guest-anchor-offset">
        html.sp-guest-anchor-offset { scroll-padding-top:var(--sp-guest-anchor-offset, 140px); }
      </style>
      <script id="smmprime-guest-anchor-navigation">
      (function () {
        var shell = document.querySelector('.sp-guest-shell');
        if (!shell) return;
        function findTarget(hash) {
          if (!hash || hash === '#') return null;
          var id;
          try { id = decodeURIComponent(hash.slice(1)); } catch (error) { return null; }
          return document.getElementById(id) || document.getElementsByName(id)[0] || null;
        }
        function move(target, smooth) {
          var top = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - shell.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - 12;
          var reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
          window.scrollTo({ top:Math.max(0, top), behavior:smooth && !reduced ? 'smooth' : 'instant' });
        }
        // Own real same-page anchors so older theme handlers cannot apply an obsolete offset.
        document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
          if (event.button !== 0 || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey || event.altKey) return;
          var link = event.target.closest ? event.target.closest('a[href]') : null;
          if (!link || link.hasAttribute('download') || (link.target && link.target !== '_self') ||
              link.hasAttribute('data-toggle') || link.hasAttribute('data-bs-toggle') || link.getAttribute('role') === 'tab') return;
          var url;
          try { url = new URL(link.href, window.location.href); } catch (error) { return; }
          if (url.origin !== location.origin || url.pathname !== location.pathname || url.search !== location.search) return;
          var target = findTarget(url.hash);
          if (!target || target.getClientRects().length === 0) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopImmediatePropagation();
          if (location.hash !== url.hash) history.pushState(null, '', url.href);
          move(target, true);
        }, true);
        function alignHash() {
          var target = findTarget(location.hash);
          if (target && target.getClientRects().length) requestAnimationFrame(function () { move(target, false); });
        }
        window.addEventListener('hashchange', alignHash);
        if (document.readyState === 'complete') alignHash();
        else window.addEventListener('load', alignHash, { once:true });
      }());
      </script>

  {% endif %}
    <script id="smmprime-mobile-header-space">
      (function () {
        var header = document.querySelector('.mobile-header.sp-simple-mobile') ||
          document.querySelector('.sp-simple-public > .header__mobile');
        if (!header) return;
        var authenticated = header.classList.contains('sp-simple-mobile');
        // The guest header was already fixed in the original layout.
        // Its pages already reserve space, so do not add a second spacer.
        if (!authenticated) return;
        var media = window.matchMedia(authenticated ? '(max-width:700px)' : '(max-width:1100px)');
        var spacer = document.createElement('div');
        spacer.className = 'sp-mobile-header-space';
        spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        header.parentNode.insertBefore(spacer, header);
        function update() {
          spacer.style.display = media.matches ? 'block' : 'none';
          spacer.style.height = media.matches ? Math.ceil(header.getBoundingClientRect().height) + 'px' : '0px';
        }
        update();
        if ('ResizeObserver' in window) new ResizeObserver(update).observe(header);
        window.addEventListener('resize', update);
        window.addEventListener('pageshow', update);
        window.addEventListener('load', update);
        if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(update);
      }());
    </script>

<script id="sp-integration-accessible-name">
(function () {
  'use strict';
  function labelTelegram() {
    var links = document.querySelectorAll('a.telegram-button');
    for (var i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
      var link = links[i];
      if (/^https?:\/\/(?:telegram\.me|t\.me)\/SmPrim\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(link.href) &&
          !link.getAttribute('aria-label') && !link.getAttribute('aria-labelledby') &&
          !link.textContent.trim()) {
        link.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Telegram-канал SMMPrime');
      }
    }
  }
  labelTelegram();
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', labelTelegram, {once:true});
  if (window.MutationObserver) {
    var observer = new MutationObserver(labelTelegram);
    observer.observe(document.body, {childList:true, subtree:true});
    window.setTimeout(function () { observer.disconnect(); }, 15000);
  }
}());
</script>

<script id="sp-mobile-google-loading">
(function () {
  'use strict';
  function setup() {
    var marker = document.querySelector('[data-sp-google-src]');
    if (!marker) return;
    var state = 'idle';
    var observer;
    function loadGoogle() {
      if (state !== 'idle') return;
      state = 'loading';
      var script = document.createElement('script');
      script.async = true;
      script.src = marker.getAttribute('data-sp-google-src');
      script.onload = function () {
        state = 'loaded';
        if (observer) observer.disconnect();
      };
      script.onerror = function () {
        state = 'idle';
        script.remove();
      };
      document.head.appendChild(script);
    }
    var form = document.getElementById('signIn');
    var mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)');
    if (!mobile.matches || !form || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      loadGoogle();
      return;
    }
    observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      if (entries.some(function (entry) { return entry.isIntersecting; })) loadGoogle();
    }, {rootMargin:'800px 0px'});
    observer.observe(form);
    form.addEventListener('focusin', loadGoogle);
    form.addEventListener('pointerdown', loadGoogle, {passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var link = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('a[href]');
      if (link && link.hash === '#signIn') loadGoogle();
    }, true);
    function checkHash() { if (location.hash === '#signIn') loadGoogle(); }
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', checkHash);
    checkHash();
    function checkWidth() { if (!mobile.matches) loadGoogle(); }
    if (mobile.addEventListener) mobile.addEventListener('change', checkWidth);
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setup, {once:true});
  else setup();
}());
</script>
{% endif %}
{% if spGuestHome %}
<script id="sp-platform-loader-v12">
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var markers = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-sp-platform-src]'));
  var state = 'idle', next = 0, pendingSubmit = null, notice;
  var form = document.querySelector('#signIn form');
  var mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)').matches;
  function clearNotice() { if (notice) { notice.remove(); notice = null; } }
  function failed() {
    state = 'failed';
    clearNotice();
    if (!form) return;
    notice = document.createElement('button');
    notice.type = 'button';
    notice.textContent = 'Не удалось загрузить форму входа. Повторить';
    notice.addEventListener('click', start);
    form.appendChild(notice);
  }
  function complete() {
    try {
      window.spApplyGuestPlatformConfig();
      if (window.jQuery) window.jQuery.holdReady(false);
      state = 'ready';
      clearNotice();
      if (pendingSubmit) {
        var request = pendingSubmit;
        pendingSubmit = null;
        if (request.form.requestSubmit) request.form.requestSubmit(request.submitter || undefined);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      failed();
      console.error('SMMPrime: platform initialization failed', error);
    }
  }
  function loadNext() {
    if (next >= markers.length) { complete(); return; }
    var script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = markers[next].getAttribute('data-sp-platform-src');
    script.async = false;
    script.onload = function () { next++; loadNext(); };
    script.onerror = function () { script.remove(); failed(); };
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  }
  function start() {
    if (state === 'loading' || state === 'ready') return;
    state = 'loading';
    clearNotice();
    // Fetch together, execute in source order. A failed dependency stops the chain.
    markers.slice(next).forEach(function (marker) {
      if (marker.getAttribute('data-sp-prefetched')) return;
      marker.setAttribute('data-sp-prefetched', '1');
      var preload = document.createElement('link');
      preload.rel = 'preload'; preload.as = 'script';
      preload.href = marker.getAttribute('data-sp-platform-src');
      document.head.appendChild(preload);
    });
    loadNext();
  }
  if (form) {
    form.addEventListener('focusin', start);
    form.addEventListener('pointerdown', start, {passive:true});
    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
      if (state === 'ready') return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      pendingSubmit = {form:form, submitter:event.submitter};
      start();
    }, true);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    var link = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('a[href]');
    if (link && link.hash === '#signIn') start();
  }, true);
  function startAfterPaint() {
    if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden' || !window.requestAnimationFrame) { start(); return; }
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(start); });
  }
  // This is automatic loading after a paint, not a delay until interaction.
  if (!mobile || location.hash === '#signIn') start();
  else if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', startAfterPaint, {once:true});
  else startAfterPaint();
}());

</script>
{% endif %}
  </body>
